-- [3] ตัวช่วย UI
---------------------------------------------------------------------
local B64CHARS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local function b64decode(s)
    s = s:gsub("%s", "")
    local lut = {}
    for i = 1, #B64CHARS do lut[B64CHARS:byte(i)] = i - 1 end
    local out, k = {}, 0
    for i = 1, #s, 4 do
        local a, b, c, d = s:byte(i, i + 3)
        local n = lut[a] * 262144 + lut[b] * 4096 + (lut[c] or 0) * 64 + (lut[d] or 0)
        local b1 = math.floor(n / 65536)
        local b2 = math.floor(n / 256) % 256
        local b3 = n % 256
        k = k + 1
        if c == 61 then
            out[k] = string.char(b1)
        elseif d == 61 then
            out[k] = string.char(b1, b2)
        else
            out[k] = string.char(b1, b2, b3)
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

-- เขียนรูปที่ฝังไว้ลงไฟล์ แล้วคืนค่า asset ที่ใช้กับ Image ได้ (ถ้าทำไม่ได้คืน "")
local function loadAsset(fileName, b64)
    if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return "" end
    local result = ""
    pcall(function()
        writefile(fileName, b64decode(b64))
        result = getcustomasset(fileName)
    end)
    return result
end

-- เลือกที่วาง UI: gethui > CoreGui > PlayerGui (อยู่เหนือ UI ของเกม)
local function getUiParent()
    if gethui then
        local ok, h = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and h then return h end
    end
    local ok, cg = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if ok and cg then
        local okTest = pcall(function() return cg.Name end)
        if okTest then return cg end
    end
    return Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

-- Helpers
local function new(class, props, parent)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
    o.Parent = parent
    return o
end

local function corner(parent, r)
    return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, parent)
end

local function stroke(parent, th, color)
    return new("UIStroke", {
        Thickness = th,
        Color = color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
    }, parent)
end

-- ไล่เฉดสีโลหะเงิน
local function metal(parent, rot)
    return new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = rot or 45,
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(240, 241, 245)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, Color3.fromRGB(130, 132, 138)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, Color3.fromRGB(200, 202, 208)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(95, 97, 103)),
        }),
    }, parent)
end

-- เพชรสี่เหลี่ยมขนมเปียกปูน (เหมือนในโลโก้)
local function diamond(parent, size, pos, z)
    local d = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = pos,
        Rotation = 45,
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = z or 5,
    }, parent)
    metal(d, 45)
    stroke(d, 1, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 14))
    return d
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- [3.1] ลาก/แตะได้ (แตะ = onTap, ลาก = ย้ายตำแหน่ง ไม่เด้งเปิดเมนู)
--       เพิ่มพารามิเตอร์ conns เพื่อเก็บ connection ไว้ปิดตอน Unload
---------------------------------------------------------------------
local function makeDraggable(handle, target, onTap, conns)
    local dragging, moved, dragStart, startPos = false, false, nil, nil
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging, moved = true, false
            dragStart, startPos = input.Position, target.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    if not moved and onTap then onTap() end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    local moveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            if d.Magnitude > 6 then moved = true end
            if moved then
                target.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end
    end)
    if conns then table.insert(conns, moveConn) end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- [4] NapUI : หน้าต่าง / แท็บ / สวิตช์ / ดรอปดาวน์ / ช่องกรอก / ปุ่ม
--     ธีม Metal Dragon  (ชื่อเมธอดตรงกับ Fluent ที่ต้นฉบับเรียกใช้)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
local NapUI = {
    Themes = { "Dark", "Amethyst", "Crimson", "Aqua" },
    Unloaded = false,
    GUI = nil,
}

local THEME_ACCENT = {
    Dark = Color3.fromRGB(215, 216, 222),
    Amethyst = Color3.fromRGB(190, 60, 255),
    Crimson = Color3.fromRGB(235, 70, 80),
    Aqua = Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 225),
}

local COLOR = {
    Text = Color3.fromRGB(235, 236, 240),
    Sub = Color3.fromRGB(165, 167, 176),
    Row = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 25),
    Line = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 76),
    Off = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 53),
    Dark = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 33),
    Knob = Color3.fromRGB(200, 202, 208),
}

local uiState = {
    accent = THEME_ACCENT.Dark,
    hooks = {},
    conns = {},
    toasts = nil,
    toastN = 0,
}

local function trackConn(conn)
    table.insert(uiState.conns, conn)
    return conn
end

-- ลงทะเบียนฟังก์ชันที่ต้องวาดสีใหม่เมื่อเปลี่ยนธีม (เรียกครั้งแรกทันที)
local function onTheme(fn)
    table.insert(uiState.hooks, fn)
    fn()
end

local function safeCall(fn, ...)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
    if not ok then warn("[NAPHUB UI]", err) end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- แท็บ (หน้าเนื้อหา 1 หน้า)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
local function createTab(window, title)
    local tab = { Title = title }
    local order = 0
    local function nextOrder()
        order = order + 1
        return order
    end

    local page = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = "Page_" .. tostring(title),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ScrollBarThickness = 3,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
        ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        Visible = false,
    }, window.PageHolder)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, page)
    new("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
    }, page)
    tab.Page = page

    local function makeRow(height, plain, parent, layoutOrder)
        local row = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.Row,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.25,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or nextOrder(),
        }, parent or page)
        corner(row, 8)
        if plain then
            metal(stroke(row, 1.5, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 45)
        else
            stroke(row, 1, COLOR.Line)
        end
        return row
    end

    local function rowTitle(row, text, size)
        return new("TextLabel", {
            Size = size or UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(text or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
            TextSize = 14,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, row)
    end

    -- หัวข้อกลุ่ม
    function tab:AddSection(text)
        local f = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
        }, page)
        diamond(f, 7, UDim2.fromOffset(9, 12), 5)
        new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(text or ""),
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 226, 232),
            TextSize = 13,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        }, f)
        return tab
    end

    -- แถวข้อมูล (ชื่อซ้าย / ค่าชิดขวา) ใช้แสดงข้อมูลอย่างเดียว ไม่มีปุ่ม
    function tab:AddInfo(id, cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local row = makeRow(30)
        rowTitle(row, cfg.Title, UDim2.new(0.45, -12, 1, 0))
        local val = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.55, -14, 1, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.Default or "-"),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            TextSize = 13,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, row)
        local o = { Type = "Info" }
        function o:SetValue(text)
            val.Text = tostring(text)
        end
        return o
    end

    -- การ์ดผู้เล่น (รูปโปรไฟล์ + ชื่อ + ไอดี)
    function tab:AddPlayerCard(cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local row = makeRow(76, true)
        local box = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 60),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, row)
        corner(box, 10)
        local img = new("ImageLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = "",
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        }, box)
        corner(img, 10)
        local nameL = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 24),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 14),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.Title or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
            TextSize = 17,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, row)
        local subL = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 18),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 40),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.Sub or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            TextSize = 12,
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, row)
        local o = { Type = "PlayerCard" }
        function o:SetImage(image) img.Image = image or "" end
        function o:SetTexts(a, b)
            nameL.Text = tostring(a or "")
            subL.Text = tostring(b or "")
        end
        return o
    end

    -- หน้าต่างข้อมูลผู้เล่นแบบรวมเป็นกรอบเดียว (หัวการ์ด + หัวข้อ + แถวข้อมูล)
    function tab:AddPlayerPanel(cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local panel = makeRow(10, true)
        panel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, panel)
        new("UIPadding", {
            PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10),
            PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12),
        }, panel)
        local n = 0
        local function ord()
            n = n + 1
            return n
        end

        local head = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 68),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder = ord(),
        }, panel)
        local box = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 60),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, head)
        corner(box, 10)
        local img = new("ImageLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = "",
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        }, box)
        corner(img, 10)
        local nameL = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 24),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 10),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.Title or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
            TextSize = 17,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, head)
        local subL = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 18),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 38),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.Sub or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            TextSize = 12,
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, head)

        local p = { Type = "PlayerPanel" }
        function p:SetImage(image) img.Image = image or "" end
        function p:SetTexts(a, b)
            nameL.Text = tostring(a or "")
            subL.Text = tostring(b or "")
        end
        function p:AddHeading(text)
            new("TextLabel", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = tostring(text),
                TextColor3 = uiState.accent,
                TextSize = 12,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                LayoutOrder = ord(),
            }, panel)
        end
        function p:AddStat(label)
            local r = new("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                LayoutOrder = ord(),
            }, panel)
            new("TextLabel", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = tostring(label),
                TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
                TextSize = 13,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            }, r)
            local v = new("TextLabel", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = "-",
                TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
                TextSize = 13,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            }, r)
            return { SetValue = function(_, text) v.Text = tostring(text) end }
        end
        return p
    end

    -- สวิตช์เปิด/ปิด
    function tab:AddToggle(id, cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local t = { Type = "Toggle", Value = cfg.Default == true, Changed = nil }
        local row = makeRow(cfg.Desc and 64 or 38)
        if cfg.Desc then
            local tl = rowTitle(row, cfg.Title, UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 22))
            tl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 8)
            tl.TextSize = 15
            tl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            new("TextLabel", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 28),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 32),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = tostring(cfg.Desc),
                TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
                TextSize = 12,
                Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
                TextWrapped = true,
            }, row)
        else
            rowTitle(row, cfg.Title)
        end

        local track = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 20),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.Off,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, row)
        corner(track, 10)
        stroke(track, 1, COLOR.Line)
        local knob = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.Knob,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, track)
        corner(knob, 7)

        local function render(animate)
            local on = t.Value
            local knobPos = on and UDim2.new(1, -17, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0)
            local knobColor = on and COLOR.Dark or COLOR.Knob
            local trackColor = on and uiState.accent or COLOR.Off
            if animate then
                local info = TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
                TweenService:Create(knob, info, { Position = knobPos, BackgroundColor3 = knobColor }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(track, info, { BackgroundColor3 = trackColor }):Play()
            else
                knob.Position = knobPos
                knob.BackgroundColor3 = knobColor
                track.BackgroundColor3 = trackColor
            end
        end

        local hit = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            ZIndex = 3,
        }, row)
        hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            t:SetValue(not t.Value)
        end)

        function t:SetValue(v)
            self.Value = not not v
            render(true)
            safeCall(self.Changed, self.Value)
        end
        function t:OnChanged(fn)
            self.Changed = fn
            safeCall(fn, self.Value)
        end

        onTheme(function() render(false) end)
        return t
    end

    -- ปุ่มกด
    function tab:AddButton(cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local row = makeRow(36, true)
        local btn = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.Title or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
            TextSize = 14,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
        }, row)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 88)
            TweenService:Create(row, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.Row }):Play()
            safeCall(cfg.Callback)
        end)
        return { Type = "Button" }
    end

    -- ช่องกรอก (Numeric / Finished เหมือน Fluent)
    function tab:AddInput(id, cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local inp = {
            Type = "Input",
            Numeric = cfg.Numeric == true,
            Finished = cfg.Finished == true,
            Value = tostring(cfg.Default == nil and "" or cfg.Default),
            Changed = nil,
        }
        local row = makeRow(38)
        rowTitle(row, cfg.Title, UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 1, 0))

        local box = new("TextBox", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 26),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = inp.Value,
            PlaceholderText = tostring(cfg.Placeholder or ""),
            PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 122, 130),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
            TextSize = 13,
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        }, row)
        corner(box, 6)
        stroke(box, 1, COLOR.Line)
        new("UIPadding", {
            PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
            PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
        }, box)

        local busy = false
        function inp:SetValue(text)
            text = tostring(text == nil and "" or text)
            if cfg.MaxLength and #text > cfg.MaxLength then
                text = text:sub(1, cfg.MaxLength)
            end
            if self.Numeric and (not tonumber(text)) and #text > 0 then
                text = self.Value
            end
            self.Value = text
            busy = true
            box.Text = text
            busy = false
            safeCall(self.Changed, self.Value)
        end
        function inp:OnChanged(fn)
            self.Changed = fn
            safeCall(fn, self.Value)
        end

        box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            if inp.Finished then inp:SetValue(box.Text) end
        end)
        box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if busy or inp.Finished then return end
            if box.Text == inp.Value then return end
            inp:SetValue(box.Text)
        end)
        return inp
    end

    -- ดรอปดาวน์ (เลือกเดี่ยว / เลือกหลายอัน) กางลงในหน้าเดียวกัน
    function tab:AddDropdown(id, cfg)
        cfg = cfg or {}
        local dd = {
            Type = "Dropdown",
            Values = cfg.Values or {},
            Multi = cfg.Multi == true,
            Value = nil,
            Changed = nil,
            Labels = cfg.Labels or {},
        }
        local function disp(v)
            return (dd.Labels and dd.Labels[v]) or tostring(v)
        end
        if dd.Multi then
            dd.Value = {}
            if type(cfg.Default) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(cfg.Default) do
                    if type(k) == "number" then
                        dd.Value[v] = true
                    elseif v then
                        dd.Value[k] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif cfg.Default ~= nil and table.find(dd.Values, cfg.Default) then
            dd.Value = cfg.Default
        end

        local holder = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
        }, page)
        new("UIListLayout", {
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        }, holder)

        local row = makeRow(38, false, holder, 1)
        rowTitle(row, cfg.Title, UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 1, 0))
        local valueLabel = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -34, 1, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "ยังไม่ได้เลือก",
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            TextSize = 13,
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, row)
        local arrow = diamond(row, 7, UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), 4)

        local listFrame = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 120),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Visible = false,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            LayoutOrder = 2,
        }, holder)
        corner(listFrame, 8)
        stroke(listFrame, 1, COLOR.Line)
        local listScroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ScrollBarThickness = 3,
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        }, listFrame)
        new("UIListLayout", {
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 3),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        }, listScroll)
        local emptyLabel = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "ไม่มีตัวเลือก",
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            TextSize = 12,
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            Visible = false,
        }, listScroll)

        local options = {}
        local shown = {}
        local opened = false

        local function isSelected(v)
            if dd.Multi then return dd.Value[v] == true end
            return dd.Value == v
        end
        local function refreshMarks()
            for _, o in ipairs(options) do
                local s = isSelected(o.value)
                o.mark.Visible = s
                o.mark.BackgroundColor3 = uiState.accent
                o.label.TextColor3 = s and uiState.accent or COLOR.Text
            end
        end
        local function refreshLabel()
            local text
            if dd.Multi then
                local names = {}
                for _, v in ipairs(dd.Values) do
                    if dd.Value[v] then table.insert(names, disp(v)) end
                end
                text = #names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or "ยังไม่ได้เลือก"
            else
                text = dd.Value ~= nil and disp(dd.Value) or "ยังไม่ได้เลือก"
            end
            valueLabel.Text = text
        end
        local refreshInfo
        local function setOpen(o)
            opened = o
            listFrame.Visible = o
            if o and refreshInfo then pcall(refreshInfo) end
            arrow.BackgroundColor3 = o and uiState.accent or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        end
        local function pick(v)
            if dd.Multi then
                local nv = {}
                for k, b in pairs(dd.Value) do nv[k] = b end
                if nv[v] then nv[v] = nil else nv[v] = true end
                dd:SetValue(nv)
            else
                dd:SetValue(v)
                setOpen(false)
            end
        end
        refreshInfo = function()
            if not cfg.GetInfo then return end
            for _, o in ipairs(options) do
                if o.info then
                    local ok, text, dim = pcall(cfg.GetInfo, o.value)
                    if ok then
                        o.info.Text = tostring(text or "")
                        o.info.TextColor3 = dim and COLOR.Sub or COLOR.Text
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local function buildList()
            for _, o in ipairs(options) do o.button:Destroy() end
            options = {}
            shown = {}
            local rowH = cfg.GetIcon and 40 or 28
            for i, v in ipairs(dd.Values) do
                shown[i] = v
                local b = new("TextButton", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rowH),
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 38),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Text = "",
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    LayoutOrder = i,
                }, listScroll)
                corner(b, 6)
                local mark = new("Frame", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.6, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = uiState.accent,
                    Visible = false,
                }, b)
                corner(mark, 2)

                -- รูปไอเท็ม (ถ้ามี)
                local textX = 10
                if cfg.GetIcon then
                    local okI, sprite = pcall(cfg.GetIcon, v)
                    if okI and type(sprite) == "table" and sprite.image then
                        local box = new("Frame", {
                            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32),
                            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                            Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0),
                            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18),
                            BorderSizePixel = 0,
                        }, b)
                        corner(box, 6)
                        new("ImageLabel", {
                            Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4),
                            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2),
                            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                            Image = sprite.image,
                            ImageRectOffset = sprite.offset or Vector2.new(),
                            ImageRectSize = sprite.size or Vector2.new(),
                            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                        }, box)
                        textX = 48
                    end
                end

                -- ข้อมูลต่อท้าย เช่น จำนวนสต็อก (ชิดขวา)
                local infoLabel
                local infoW = 0
                if cfg.GetInfo then
                    infoW = cfg.InfoWidth or 84
                    infoLabel = new("TextLabel", {
                        Size = UDim2.new(0, infoW, 1, 0),
                        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
                        Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Text = "",
                        TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
                        TextSize = 12,
                        Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
                        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                    }, b)
                end

                local lbl = new("TextLabel", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -(textX + infoW + 8), 1, 0),
                    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(textX, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Text = disp(v),
                    TextColor3 = COLOR.Text,
                    TextSize = 13,
                    Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                }, b)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pick(v) end)
                table.insert(options, { value = v, button = b, mark = mark, label = lbl, info = infoLabel })
            end
            emptyLabel.Visible = #dd.Values == 0
            listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.clamp(#dd.Values * (rowH + 3) + 8, 36, cfg.GetIcon and 210 or 160))
            refreshMarks()
            refreshInfo()
        end
        function dd:RefreshInfo()
            refreshInfo()
        end
        function dd:Rebuild()
            buildList()
        end

        function dd:SetValue(val)
            if self.Multi then
                local nv = {}
                if type(val) == "table" then
                    for k, b in pairs(val) do
                        if b and table.find(self.Values, k) then nv[k] = true end
                    end
                end
                self.Value = nv
            else
                if val == nil then
                    self.Value = nil
                elseif table.find(self.Values, val) then
                    self.Value = val
                end
            end
            refreshMarks()
            refreshLabel()
            safeCall(self.Changed, self.Value)
        end
        function dd:SetValues(newValues)
            if newValues then self.Values = newValues end
            local same = #self.Values == #shown
            if same then
                for i, v in ipairs(self.Values) do
                    if shown[i] ~= v then
                        same = false
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not same then buildList() end
            refreshLabel()
        end
        function dd:OnChanged(fn)
            self.Changed = fn
            safeCall(fn, self.Value)
        end

        local hit = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            ZIndex = 3,
        }, row)
        hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setOpen(not opened) end)

        buildList()
        refreshLabel()
        onTheme(function()
            refreshMarks()
            arrow.BackgroundColor3 = opened and uiState.accent or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        end)
        return dd
    end

    return tab
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- หน้าต่างหลัก (ดีไซน์ Metal Dragon จากไฟล์ UI)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
function NapUI:CreateWindow(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}

    -- เคลียร์ UI เก่า กันซ้อน + รีเซ็ตสถานะ
    for _, c in ipairs(uiState.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    uiState.conns = {}
    uiState.hooks = {}
    self.Unloaded = false
    if THEME_ACCENT[cfg.Theme] then uiState.accent = THEME_ACCENT[cfg.Theme] end

    local UiParent = getUiParent()
    local playerGui = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    for _, root in pairs({ playerGui, UiParent }) do
        if root then
            for _, name in ipairs({ "NAPHUBUI", "NopeHubAntEmpire", "NAPHUBToggle" }) do
                pcall(function()
                    local old = root:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if old then old:Destroy() end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local ScreenGui = new("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "NAPHUBUI",
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = 2147483647, -- ซ้อนบนสุด เหนือเมนูของเกม
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    }, UiParent)
    self.GUI = ScreenGui

    -- Main Window
    local MainFrame = new("Frame", {
        Name = "MainFrame",
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.7, 0.85),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 22),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = true,
    }, ScreenGui)
    corner(MainFrame, 14)
    new("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(580, 360) }, MainFrame)
    local MainScale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, MainFrame)

    -- ขอบโลหะนอก
    local rim = stroke(MainFrame, 3, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
    metal(rim, 45)

    -- แผ่นโลหะด้านใน
    local Plate = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Plate",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 39),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, MainFrame)
    corner(Plate, 10)
    stroke(Plate, 1, Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 76))
    new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(52, 52, 56), Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 31)),
    }, Plate)

    -- รูปพื้นหลังเมนู ถ้าโหลดไม่ได้จะใช้พื้นเทาเดิม
    local bgAsset = loadAsset("naphub_bg_v1.jpg", BG_B64)
    if bgAsset ~= "" then
        local BgImage = new("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "BgImage",
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = bgAsset,
            ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150), -- ปรับความมืดของพื้นหลัง (ใหญ่ = สว่างขึ้น)
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
            ZIndex = 0,
        }, Plate)
        corner(BgImage, 10)
    end

    -- Header (ลากหน้าต่างจากตรงนี้)
    local Header = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Header",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.35,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, Plate)
    corner(Header, 10)
    diamond(Header, 9, UDim2.fromOffset(20, 21), 6)

    new("TextLabel", {
        Name = "TitleShadow",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 2),
        Text = "NAPHUB",
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        TextTransparency = 0.2,
        TextSize = 30,
        Font = Enum.Font.Fantasy,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, Header)
    local TitleLabel = new("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Title",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 0),
        Text = "NAPHUB",
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        TextSize = 30,
        Font = Enum.Font.Fantasy,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, Header)
    new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(190, 192, 200)),
        }),
    }, TitleLabel)

    -- ชื่อเกม (SubTitle) ข้างโลโก้ชื่อ
    if cfg.SubTitle and cfg.SubTitle ~= "" then
        new("TextLabel", {
            Name = "SubTitle",
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -206, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(162, 3),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = tostring(cfg.SubTitle),
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 192, 200),
            TextSize = 13,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, Header)
    end

    local CloseBtn = new("TextButton", {
        Name = "CloseBtn",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 33),
        Text = "X",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 216, 222),
        TextSize = 16,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        AutoButtonColor = true,
    }, Header)
    corner(CloseBtn, 8)
    metal(stroke(CloseBtn, 1.5, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 45)

    -- เส้นคั่นไล่เฉดมีเพชรตรงกลาง
    local Divider = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 2),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 42),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, Plate)
    new("UIGradient", {
        Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(215, 216, 222), Color3.fromRGB(215, 216, 222)),
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        }),
    }, Divider)
    diamond(Divider, 8, UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), 6)

    -- พื้นที่เนื้อหา
    local Content = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Content",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -64),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 52),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
    }, Plate)
    corner(Content, 8)
    stroke(Content, 1, Color3.fromRGB(62, 62, 68))

    -- แถบรายชื่อแท็บ (ซ้าย) + เส้นคั่น + หน้าเนื้อหา (ขวา)
    local Sidebar = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = "Sidebar",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 1, -8),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 4),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ScrollBarThickness = 0,
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
        ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
    }, Content)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, Sidebar)
    new("Frame", {
        Name = "SideLine",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -16),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(129, 8),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.Line,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, Content)
    local PageHolder = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Pages",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -138, 1, -8),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(134, 4),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
    }, Content)

    -- เพชรประดับกลางขอบทั้ง 4 ด้าน (เหมือนโลโก้)
    diamond(MainFrame, 12, UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), 8)
    diamond(MainFrame, 12, UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1), 8)
    diamond(MainFrame, 12, UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5), 8)
    diamond(MainFrame, 12, UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.5), 8)

    makeDraggable(Header, MainFrame, nil, uiState.conns)

    local function setOpen(open)
        if open then
            MainScale.Scale = 0.92
            MainFrame.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(MainScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
        else
            MainFrame.Visible = false
        end
    end
    CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setOpen(false)
    end)

    -- ที่แสดงแจ้งเตือน (มุมขวาล่าง)
    uiState.toasts = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Toasts",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 1, -24),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ZIndex = 50,
    }, ScreenGui)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
        HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
    }, uiState.toasts)

    -- Window object (เมธอดเหมือน Fluent)
    local window = { Tabs = {}, PageHolder = PageHolder, Gui = ScreenGui }

    function window:SelectTab(i)
        local t = self.Tabs[i]
        if not t then return end
        for _, o in ipairs(self.Tabs) do
            local sel = (o == t)
            o.Page.Visible = sel
            o.Bar.Visible = sel
            o.Button.TextColor3 = sel and COLOR.Text or COLOR.Sub
            o.Button.BackgroundTransparency = sel and 0.05 or 0.4
        end
        self.Selected = i
    end

    function window:AddTab(tcfg)
        tcfg = tcfg or {}
        local tab = createTab(self, tcfg.Title)
        local index = #self.Tabs + 1

        local btn = new("TextButton", {
            Name = "Tab_" .. tostring(tcfg.Title),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.Row,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = tostring(tcfg.Title or ""),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
            TextSize = 13,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            LayoutOrder = index,
        }, Sidebar)
        corner(btn, 6)
        new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6) }, btn)
        local bar = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.6, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, -12, 0.5, 0),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Visible = false,
        }, btn)
        corner(bar, 2)
        onTheme(function() bar.BackgroundColor3 = uiState.accent end)

        tab.Button = btn
        tab.Bar = bar
        table.insert(self.Tabs, tab)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            window:SelectTab(index)
        end)
        if index == 1 then window:SelectTab(1) end
        return tab
    end

    -- แตะ = เปิด/ปิดเมนู
    function window:Minimize()
        setOpen(not MainFrame.Visible)
    end

    if cfg.MinimizeKey then
        trackConn(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if not processed and input.KeyCode == cfg.MinimizeKey then
                window:Minimize()
            end
        end))
    end

    return window
end

function NapUI:SetTheme(name)
    local a = THEME_ACCENT[name]
    if not a then return end
    uiState.accent = a
    for _, fn in ipairs(uiState.hooks) do pcall(fn) end
end

function NapUI:Notify(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    local holder = uiState.toasts
    if not holder or not holder.Parent then return end
    uiState.toastN = uiState.toastN + 1
    local toast = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 25),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        LayoutOrder = uiState.toastN,
    }, holder)
    corner(toast, 10)
    metal(stroke(toast, 2, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 45)
    new("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10),
    }, toast)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 2),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, toast)
    new("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = tostring(cfg.Title or ""),
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        TextSize = 14,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        LayoutOrder = 1,
    }, toast)
    new("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = tostring(cfg.Content or ""),
        TextColor3 = COLOR.Sub,
        TextSize = 12,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
        TextWrapped = true,
        LayoutOrder = 2,
    }, toast)
    task.delay(tonumber(cfg.Duration) or 3, function()
        pcall(function() toast:Destroy() end)
    end)
end

function NapUI:Destroy()
    self.Unloaded = true
    for _, c in ipairs(uiState.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    uiState.conns = {}
    if self.GUI then
        pcall(function() self.GUI:Destroy() end)
    end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- [5] ลูกลอย (Floating Button) ใช้รูปโลโก้ NAP จริง
--     คืนค่า (gui, cleanup) เหมือนฟังก์ชันลูกลอยเดิมของต้นฉบับ
---------------------------------------------------------------------
function NapUI.CreateFloatingButton(window, parent)
    local gui = new("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "NAPHUBToggle",
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = 2147483647,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    }, parent)
    local conns = {}

    local BTN_SIZE = 48 -- ขนาดลูกลอย (พิกเซล) ปรับเลขนี้ได้
    local FloatingBtn = new("TextButton", {
        Name = "FloatingToggleButton",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BTN_SIZE, BTN_SIZE),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -BTN_SIZE / 2),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Active = true,
    }, gui)

    -- แบบวาดเอง (สำรอง ใช้เมื่อโหลดรูปไม่ได้)
    local Fallback = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Fallback",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 37),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, FloatingBtn)
    corner(Fallback, 14)
    new("UIScale", { Scale = BTN_SIZE / 64 }, Fallback)
    new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 62), Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 24)),
    }, Fallback)
    metal(stroke(Fallback, 3, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 45)

    local Inner = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, Fallback)
    corner(Inner, 10)
    stroke(Inner, 1, Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 92))

    local Ring1 = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, Fallback)
    corner(Ring1, 23)
    metal(stroke(Ring1, 2.5, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 135)

    local NapText = new("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Text = "NAP",
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        TextSize = 19,
        Font = Enum.Font.Fantasy,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ZIndex = 3,
    }, Fallback)
    metal(NapText, 90)

    diamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -23), 4)
    diamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 23), 4)
    diamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, -23, 0.5, 0), 4)
    diamond(Fallback, 8, UDim2.new(0.5, 23, 0.5, 0), 4)

    -- รูปโลโก้จริง
    local logoImage = LOGO_IMAGE
    if logoImage == "" then
        logoImage = loadAsset("naphub_logo_v3.png", LOGO_B64)
    end

    if logoImage ~= "" then
        local Img = new("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "Logo",
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = logoImage,
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
            ZIndex = 10,
        }, FloatingBtn)
        task.spawn(function()
            local t = 0
            while not Img.IsLoaded and t < 5 do
                task.wait(0.1)
                t = t + 0.1
            end
            if Img.IsLoaded then
                Fallback.Visible = false
            else
                Img:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end

    -- แตะ = เปิด/ปิดเมนู | ลาก = ย้ายลูกลอย
    makeDraggable(FloatingBtn, FloatingBtn, function()
        window:Minimize()
    end, conns)

    return gui, function()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
    end
end



---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ค้นหา ecsWorld / replicatedState ของเกม (ลองซ้ำ + สแกนลึกขึ้น + สำรองด้วย getgc)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
function NapUI.FindReplication(rs)
    local ecs, rep
    local diag = {}

    local function look(t, raw)
        if type(t) ~= "table" then return end
        local ok1, a = pcall(function() if raw then return rawget(t, "_storages") end return t._storages end)
        if ok1 and a ~= nil and not ecs then ecs = t end
        local function get(tbl, k)
            local ok, v = pcall(function() if raw then return rawget(tbl, k) end return tbl[k] end)
            return ok and v or nil
        end
        if not rep then
            if get(t, "LocalPlayerAttributes") ~= nil then
                rep = t
            else
                local core = get(t, "Core")
                if type(core) == "table" and (get(core, "DungeonClient") ~= nil or get(core, "StarEventClient") ~= nil) then
                    rep = t
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local seen = {}
    local function scanFn(f, depth)
        if type(f) ~= "function" or seen[f] or depth > 3 then return end
        seen[f] = true
        local ok, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, f)
        if not ok or type(ups) ~= "table" then return end
        for _, v in pairs(ups) do
            if type(v) == "table" then
                look(v, false)
                pcall(function()
                    for _, v2 in pairs(v) do
                        if type(v2) == "table" then look(v2, true) end
                    end
                end)
            elseif type(v) == "function" then
                scanFn(v, depth + 1)
            end
        end
    end

    local remote
    pcall(function() remote = rs:WaitForChild("MatterDiff", 5) end)
    diag[#diag + 1] = "MatterDiff remote: " .. tostring(remote ~= nil)
    diag[#diag + 1] = "getconnections: " .. tostring(getconnections ~= nil) .. " | getgc: " .. tostring(getgc ~= nil)

    pcall(function()
        local m = require(rs.Battle.Game.clientReplication)
        if type(m) == "table" then
            look(m, false)
            for _, v in pairs(m) do
                if type(v) == "table" then look(v, false) end
            end
        end
    end)

    local connDiag = "connections: ไม่พบ"
    for attempt = 1, 15 do
        if remote and getconnections then
            local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, remote.OnClientEvent)
            if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
                local withFn = 0
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
                    local fn = c.Function
                    if fn then withFn = withFn + 1 scanFn(fn, 0) end
                end
                connDiag = "connections: " .. #conns .. " (มี Function " .. withFn .. ") ลองครั้งที่ " .. attempt
            end
        end
        if ecs and rep then break end
        task.wait(1)
    end

    diag[#diag + 1] = connDiag
    if not (ecs and rep) and getgc then
        local ok, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
        if ok and type(gc) == "table" then
            for _, v in ipairs(gc) do
                if type(v) == "table" then look(v, true) end
                if ecs and rep then break end
            end
        end
        diag[#diag + 1] = "getgc scan done"
    end

    diag[#diag + 1] = "ecsWorld: " .. tostring(ecs ~= nil) .. " | replicatedState: " .. tostring(rep ~= nil)
    return ecs, rep, table.concat(diag, "\n")
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- แสดง error บนหน้าจอ (ไว้ตรวจสอบเวลาเมนูไม่ขึ้น)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
function NapUI.ShowError(msg)
    local gui = new("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "NAPHUBError", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = 2147483647, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    }, getUiParent())
    local f = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 190), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 22), BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, gui)
    corner(f, 10)
    metal(stroke(f, 2, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), 45)
    new("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 6), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "NAPHUB: โหลดไม่สำเร็จ (ส่งข้อความนี้ให้ผู้ช่วย)", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120),
        TextSize = 14, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
    }, f)
    local sc = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -72), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
    }, f)
    new("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = tostring(msg), TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 235), TextSize = 12, Font = Enum.Font.Code,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true,
    }, sc)
    local copy = new("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -34), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 44),
        Text = "คัดลอก", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextSize = 14, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
    }, f)
    corner(copy, 6)
    copy.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, tostring(msg)) end
    end)
    local close = new("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 1, -34), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 44),
        Text = "ปิด", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextSize = 14, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
    }, f)
    corner(close, 6)
    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() gui:Destroy() end)
end

	repeat
		task.wait()

	if makefolder then

			"NopeHub",

		}) do
			pcall(makefolder, _2)
		end
	end
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")

	local LocalPlayer = Players .LocalPlayer

		pcall(globalEnv .NOPE_HUB_UI_CLEANUP)
	end

		Ecs = {
			Context = GameContext
		},
		PlayerData = PlayerData
	}

	local AutoUserToolState = require(ReplicatedStorage .UI.AutoUserTool.Model.State)

	local SevenDayState = require(ReplicatedStorage .UI["7DAY"].Model.SevenDayState)
	local IndexModel = require(ReplicatedStorage .UI.Index.Model.IndexModel)

	local VineConfig = require(ReplicatedStorage .Battle.Game.Helper.Activities.Vine.Config)
	local ActivityState = require(ReplicatedStorage .UI.ActivityEvent.Model.State)
	local ClickBoostModule = require(ReplicatedStorage .Battle.Game.Dungeon.ClickBoost)
	local DungeonConfig = require(ReplicatedStorage .Battle.Game.Config.DungeonConfig)
	local ActivityScheduleConfig = require(ReplicatedStorage .Battle.Game.Helper.Activities.ActivityScheduleConfig)
	local AntFoodStore = require(ReplicatedStorage .WuKongHooks.AntFoodStore)
	local WuKong = require(ReplicatedStorage .WuKong)
	local queryBlockState = {
		Active = true,
		Blocked = 0
	}
	local originalExecuteQuery = WuKong.ExecuteQuery
	local hookedExecuteQuery
	hookedExecuteQuery = hookfunction(originalExecuteQuery, function(_z, _10, ...)
		if queryBlockState.Active and type(_10) == "string" and string.find(_10, "弹出购买窗口", 1, true) then
			queryBlockState.Blocked += 1
			return false
		end
		return hookedExecuteQuery(_z, _10, ...)
	end)
	local function restoreQueryHook()
		if queryBlockState.Active then
			queryBlockState.Active = false
			restorefunction(originalExecuteQuery)
		end
	end
	if STATE then
		STATE.onCleanup(restoreQueryHook)
	end
	local ecsWorld, replicatedState, _repDiag = NapUI.FindReplication(ReplicatedStorage)
	if not ecsWorld then
		warn("[NAPHUB] ระบบข้อมูลเกมยังไม่พร้อม", _repDiag)
		pcall(NapUI.ShowError, "ระบบข้อมูลเกมยังไม่พร้อม (เมนูยังขึ้น แต่ฟีเจอร์ที่ใช้ข้อมูลเกมอาจไม่ทำงาน)\n" .. tostring(_repDiag))
	end
	if not replicatedState then
		-- ใช้เฉพาะดันเจี้ยน/กิจกรรมดาวเท่านั้น: ถ้าไม่พบ ให้ 2 ฟีเจอร์นี้ข้ามไปเฉยๆ ส่วนอื่นทำงานปกติ
		warn("[NAPHUB] ไม่พบ replicatedState (ฟีเจอร์ดันเจี้ยน/กิจกรรมดาวอาจไม่ทำงาน)", _repDiag)
		replicatedState = {}
	end
	GearShopModel:Init()
	IndexModel:Init()
	RideState:Load()
	ActivityState:Load()
	setthreadidentity(8)
	local Fluent = NapUI -- UI ใหม่ (NAPHUB Metal Dragon) แทน Fluent เดิม อยู่ในส่วน [4]
	local window = Fluent:CreateWindow({
		Title = "NAPHUB",
		SubTitle = "Build An Ant Empire | อาณาจักรมด",
		TabWidth = 160,
		Size = UDim2 .fromOffset(640, 500),
		Acrylic = false,
		Theme = "Dark",
		MinimizeKey = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift
	})
	Fluent .GUI.Name = "NopeHubAntEmpire"
	local cleanupUI = function()
		restoreQueryHook()
		setthreadidentity(8)
		pcall(function()
			Fluent:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	globalEnv .NOPE_HUB_UI_CLEANUP = cleanupUI
	if STATE then
		STATE.onCleanup(cleanupUI)
	end
	local TAB_TH = {
		["Home"] = "หน้าหลัก",
		["Autofarm"] = "ออโต้ฟาร์ม",
		["Upgrades"] = "อัปเกรด",
		["Resource Converter"] = "เครื่องแปลงทรัพยากร",
		["Trash"] = "ทิ้งมด",
		["Dungeon"] = "ดันเจี้ยน",
		["Events"] = "กิจกรรม",
		["Gear"] = "ร้านอุปกรณ์",

		["Ride"] = "พาหนะ",
		["Rewards"] = "รางวัล",
		["Settings"] = "ตั้งค่า",
		["Map"] = "แผนที่",
	}
	local tabs = {}
	for _1c, _1d in ipairs({
		{
			"Home",
			"home"
		},
		{
			"Autofarm",
			"leaf"
		},

		},

			"settings"

	}) do
		tabs[_1d[1]] = window:AddTab({
			Title = TAB_TH[_1d[1]] or _1d[1],
			Icon = _1d[2]
		})
	end
	local running = true
	local settings, controls, jobs, jobErrors, jobRuns, connections = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
	local configPath = "NopeHub/AntEmpire/" .. tostring(LocalPlayer .UserId) .. ".json"
	local loadingProfile = false
	local configData = {
		Version = 2,
		Profiles = {},
		AutoLoad = ""
	}
	if isfile(configPath) then
		local _1o, _1p = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(configPath))
		end)
		if _1o and type(_1p) == "table" then
			if _1p.Version == 2 and type(_1p.Profiles) == "table" then
				configData = _1p
			elseif _1p.Version == 1 and type(_1p.Settings) == "table" then
				configData.Profiles.Default = _1p.Settings
			end
		end
	end
	local selectedProfile, profileNameInput = nil, ""
	local profileDropdown
	local function saveConfigs()
		writefile(configPath, HttpService:JSONEncode(configData))
	end
	local function onSettingsChanged()
	end
	local function notifyConfig(_1w)
		Fluent:Notify({
			Title = "คอนฟิก",
			Content = _1w,
			Duration = 3
		})
	end
	local function refreshProfileList()
		local _1y = {}
		for _1z in pairs(configData.Profiles) do
			table.insert(_1y, _1z)
		end
		table.sort(_1y)
		if profileDropdown then
			profileDropdown:SetValues(_1y)
			if selectedProfile then
				profileDropdown:SetValue(selectedProfile)
			end
		end
	end
	local function captureSettings()
		local _21 = {}
		for _22, _23 in pairs(controls) do
			if _23 .Type == "Input" then
				_21[_22] = tonumber(_23 .Value) or settings[_22]
			else
				_21[_22] = _23 .Value
			end
		end
		return HttpService:JSONDecode(HttpService:JSONEncode(_21))
	end
	local function createProfile(_25)
		_25 = tostring(_25 or profileNameInput):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		assert(_25 ~= "" and _25 ~= "None" and # _25 <= 64, "กรุณาตั้งชื่อคอนฟิก (1-64 ตัวอักษร)")
		assert(not configData.Profiles[_25], "มีคอนฟิกชื่อนี้แล้ว ให้กด เขียนทับคอนฟิก")
		configData.Profiles[_25] = captureSettings()
		saveConfigs()
		selectedProfile = _25
		refreshProfileList()
		notifyConfig("สร้างแล้ว: " .. _25)
	end
	local function saveProfile(_27)
		_27 = _27 or selectedProfile
		assert(_27 and configData.Profiles[_27], "กรุณาเลือกคอนฟิกก่อน")
		configData.Profiles[_27] = captureSettings()
		saveConfigs()
		notifyConfig("บันทึกแล้ว: " .. _27)
	end
	local function setAutoLoad(_29)
		_29 = _29 == "None" and "" or _29
		assert(_29 == "" or configData.Profiles[_29], "ไม่พบคอนฟิกนี้")
		configData.AutoLoad = _29
		saveConfigs()
		refreshProfileList()
		notifyConfig(_29 == "" and "ปิดโหลดอัตโนมัติแล้ว" or "โหลดอัตโนมัติ: " .. _29)
	end
	local function loadProfile(_2b)
		_2b = _2b or selectedProfile
		assert(_2b and type(configData.Profiles[_2b]) == "table", "กรุณาเลือกคอนฟิกก่อน")
		local _2c = {
			Settings = configData.Profiles[_2b]
		}
		loadingProfile = true
		local _2d, _2e = pcall(function()
			setthreadidentity(8)
			for _2f, _2g in ipairs({
				false,
				true
			}) do
				for _2h, _2i in pairs(_2c.Settings) do
					if _2h == "PotionTarget" and _2i == "Selected Slots" then
						_2i = "Select Ant"
					end
					local _2j = controls[_2h]
					if _2j and (jobs[_2h] ~= nil) == _2g then
						if jobs[_2h] then
							if type(_2i) == "boolean" then
								_2j:SetValue(_2i)
							end
						elseif _2j.Values then
							if type(_2i) == "table" then
								local _2k = {}
								for _2l, _2m in pairs(_2i) do
									if _2m == true and table.find(_2j.Values, _2l) then
										_2k[_2l] = true
									end
								end
								_2j:SetValue(_2k)
							elseif table.find(_2j.Values, _2i) then
								_2j:SetValue(_2i)
							end
						elseif type(_2i) == "number" then
							_2j:SetValue(tostring(_2i))
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		loadingProfile = false
		if not _2d then
			error(_2e)
		end
		selectedProfile = _2b
		refreshProfileList()
	end
	local function isRunning()
		return running and not Fluent .Unloaded and (not STATE or STATE.alive())
	end
	local function connectTracked(_2p, _2q)
		local _2r = _2p:Connect(_2q)
		table.insert(connections, _2r)
		return _2r
	end
	local function sendCommand(_2t, _2u)
		if isRunning() then
			GameContext .SendCommand(_2t, _2u or {})
		end
	end
	local function getGold()
		return PlayerDataHelper.GetPlayerGold(LocalPlayer .UserId)
	end
	local pendingPurchase, purchaseCooldown = nil, 0
	local function tryPurchase(_2z, _30, _31)
		if not isRunning() or pendingPurchase or os.clock() < purchaseCooldown then
			return false
		end
		_30 = tonumber(_30)
		if not _30 or _30 < 0 or getGold() < _30 then
			return false
		end
		pendingPurchase = {
			Event = _2z,
			Started = os.clock()
		}
		local _32, _33 = pcall(_31)
		if not _32 or _33 == false then
			pendingPurchase = nil
			purchaseCooldown = os.clock() + 1
			if not _32 then
				error(_33)
			end
			return false
		end
		if not _2z then
			pendingPurchase = nil
			purchaseCooldown = os.clock() + 0.75
		end
		return true
	end
	local function getRoom()
		return RoomHelper.GetLocalClientRoomObject()
	end
	local function getRootPart()
		return LocalPlayer .Character and LocalPlayer .Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end
	local function teleportTo(_37)
		local _38 = getRootPart()
		if _38 and typeof(_37) == "Vector3" then
			LocalPlayer .Character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(_37 + Vector3 .new(0, 3, 0)))
			return true
		end
		return false
	end
	local function addJobToggle(_3a, _3b, _3c, _3d, _3e, _3f, _3desc)
		setthreadidentity(8)
		settings[_3b] = false
		jobs[_3b] = {
			interval = _3d,
			callback = _3e,
			nextAt = 0,
			busy = false,
			onDisable = _3f
		}
		local _3g = tabs[_3a]:AddToggle(_3b, {
			Title = _3c,
			Desc = _3desc,
			Default = false
		})
		controls[_3b] = _3g
		_3g:OnChanged(function(_3h)
			settings[_3b] = _3h == true
			jobs[_3b].nextAt = 0
			if not _3h and _3f then
				pcall(_3f)
			end
			onSettingsChanged()
		end)
	end
	local function addDropdown(_3j, _3k, _3l, _3m, _3n, _3o, _3lab, _3ext)
		setthreadidentity(8)
		settings[_3k] = _3o or (_3n and {} or nil)
		local _3cfg = {
			Title = _3l,
			Values = _3m,
			Multi = _3n,
			Labels = _3lab,
			Default = _3o or (_3n and {} or nil)
		}
		for _3ek, _3ev in pairs(_3ext or {}) do
			_3cfg[_3ek] = _3ev
		end
		local _3p = tabs[_3j]:AddDropdown(_3k, _3cfg)
		controls[_3k] = _3p
		_3p:OnChanged(function(_3q)
			settings[_3k] = _3q
			onSettingsChanged()
		end)
		return _3p
	end
	local function addNumberInput(_3s, _3t, _3u, _3v)
		setthreadidentity(8)
		settings[_3t] = _3v
		local _3w = tabs[_3s]:AddInput(_3t, {
			Title = _3u,
			Default = tostring(_3v),
			Numeric = true,
			Finished = true
		})
		controls[_3t] = _3w
		_3w:OnChanged(function(_3x)
			settings[_3t] = math.max(tonumber(_3x) or _3v, 0)
			onSettingsChanged()
		end)
	end
	local function addButton(_3z, _40, _41)
		setthreadidentity(8)
		tabs[_3z]:AddButton({
			Title = _40,
			Callback = function()
				local _42, _43 = pcall(_41)
				if not _42 then
					warn("[NAPHUB]", _40, _43)
					Fluent:Notify({
						Title = _40,
						Content = tostring(_43),
						Duration = 5
					})
				end
			end
		})
	end
	local function getDungeonSnapshot()
		local _45 = replicatedState .Core and replicatedState .Core.DungeonClient
		local _46 = _45 and _45 .EntityId
		local _47 = _46 and ecsWorld:contains(_46) and ecsWorld:get(_46, GameContext .Components.DungeonClient)
		return _47 and _47 .Snapshot, _45
	end
	-- ดึงอาหารอัตโนมัติ (Auto Get Food) ย้ายมาจากสคริปต์ต้นฉบับ: กดปุ่ม "ดึงอาหาร" ในห้องของผู้เล่นเอง
	local function getRoomModel()
		local _idx = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RoomIndex") or LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("PlayerRoomId")
		local _rooms = workspace:FindFirstChild("Rooms")
		if not _rooms then
			return nil
		end
		if _idx then
			local _r = _rooms:FindFirstChild(tostring(_idx))
			if _r then
				return _r
			end
		end
		local _kids = _rooms:GetChildren()
		return # _kids == 1 and _kids[1] or nil
	end
	local function getFoodPrompt()
		local _room = getRoomModel()
		local _root = _room and _room:FindFirstChild("PlayerFood")
		local _part = _root and _root:FindFirstChild("Prompt")
		return _part and _part:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") or nil
	end
	local function doGetFood()
		local _prompt = getFoodPrompt()
		if not _prompt then
			return
		end
		if fireproximityprompt then
			pcall(fireproximityprompt, _prompt, 0, true)
		end
		if firesignal then
			if not pcall(firesignal, _prompt.Triggered, LocalPlayer) then
				pcall(firesignal, _prompt.Triggered)
			end
		end
	end
	local foodSellPrice = nil
	table.insert(connections, EventBus .RemoteEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_4f, _4g)
		if _4f == RoomHelper.FoodSellConfig.PriceUpdateEvent and type(_4g) == "table" then
			foodSellPrice = _4g
		end
		if pendingPurchase and _4f == pendingPurchase.Event then
			pendingPurchase = nil
			purchaseCooldown = os.clock() + 0.75
		end
	end))
	local function sellFood()
		local _4i = PlayerDataHelper.GetPlayerFood(LocalPlayer .UserId)
		if (settings.BlackMarketSell or settings.AutoBlackMarket) and BlackMarket.IsSellOpen() then
			return
		end
		if _4i > 0 then
			sendCommand("SellPlayerFood", {
				FoodAmount = _4i,
				Source = "NopeHub"
			})
		end
	end
	addJobToggle("Autofarm", "AutoGetFood", "ดึงอาหารอัตโนมัติ (Auto Get Food)", 15, doGetFood)
	addNumberInput("Autofarm", "SellPrice", "ราคาขายขั้นต่ำ", 2)
	addJobToggle("Autofarm", "SellFood", "ขายอาหารอัตโนมัติ", 3, function()
		if not settings.SellByPrice then
			sellFood()
		end
	end)
	-- ===== Black Market (ย้ายตรรกะมาจากสคริปต์ Build_An_Ant_Empire.lua ที่แนบมา) =====
	local bmSells = 0
	local _bmSnapAt = 0
	local function isBlackMarketActive()
		-- ขอ snapshot จากเซิร์ฟเวอร์ทุก 15 วิ กันพลาดตอนตลาดมืดเปิด
		local _now = os.clock()
		if _now - _bmSnapAt >= 15 then
			_bmSnapAt = _now
			pcall(function()
				EventBus .FireServer("BlackMarketSnapshotRequest")
			end)
		end
		-- 1. API ทางการของเกม
		for _, _fn in ipairs({ "IsSellOpen", "IsActive" }) do
			if type(BlackMarket[_fn]) == "function" then
				local _ok, _r = pcall(BlackMarket[_fn])
				if _ok and _r == true then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
		-- 2. ตรวจจากโฟลเดอร์ใน Workspace
		if workspace:FindFirstChild("BlackMarketEvent") then
			return true
		end
		-- 3. ตรวจจากไอคอนอีเวนต์บนหน้าจอ
		local _pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		local _eg = _pg and _pg:FindFirstChild("EventGui")
		local _ic = _eg and _eg:FindFirstChild("BlackMarket Icon", true)
		if _ic and _ic.Visible and (not _ic:IsA("CanvasGroup") or _ic.GroupTransparency < 1) then
			return true
		end
		return false
	end
	local function sellToBlackMarket()
		local function _carried()
			return tonumber(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CarriedSellFoodAmount")) or 0
		end
		local _c = _carried()
		-- ตลาดมืดรับซื้อเฉพาะอาหารที่ถืออยู่: ถ้ายังไม่ได้ถือ ให้กดดึงอาหารก่อน
		if _c <= 0 then
			doGetFood()
			task.wait(0.25)
			_c = _carried()
		end
		if _c <= 0 then
			local _pool = tonumber(PlayerDataHelper.GetPlayerFood(LocalPlayer .UserId)) or 0
			if _pool <= 0 then
				return false
			end
			_c = _pool
		end
		local _sold = false
		-- 1. ผ่านฟังก์ชันทางการของเกม (เหมือนปุ่มในเกม)
		if type(BlackMarket.RequestSell) == "function" then
			local _ok, _r = pcall(BlackMarket.RequestSell)
			if _ok and _r == true then
				_sold = true
			end
		end
		-- 2. สำรอง: ส่งคำสั่งขายตรงด้วย Source = BlackMarket
		if not _sold then
			_sold = pcall(sendCommand, "SellPlayerFood", {
				FoodAmount = _c,
				Source = "BlackMarket"
			})
		end
		if _sold then
			bmSells = bmSells + 1
		end
		return _sold
	end

	local function getEquipSignature()
		local _5j = BackpackModel:GetData()
		local _5k = {}
		for _5l, _5m in pairs(_5j.Stacks or {}) do
			table.insert(_5k, "S:" .. tostring(_5l) .. ":" .. tostring(_5m))
		end
		for _5n, _5o in pairs(_5j.EquippedBySlot or {}) do

		end

			if _5q then
				table.insert(_5k, "U:" .. tostring(_5p))
			end
		end
		table.sort(_5k)
		return table.concat(_5k, "|")
	end

	local function fmtNum(_n)
		_n = tonumber(_n)
		if not _n then
			return "?"
		end
		local _units = {{1e15, "Qa"}, {1e12, "T"}, {1e9, "B"}, {1e6, "M"}, {1e3, "K"}}
		for _, _u in ipairs(_units) do
			if math.abs(_n) >= _u[1] then
				local _v = math.floor(_n / _u[1] * 100 + 0.5) / 100
				return (string.format("%.2f", _v):gsub("%.?0+$", "")) .. _u[2]
			end
		end
		return tostring(math.floor(_n + 0.5))
	end
	local _7y, _7z = {}, {}

		table.insert(_7z, _82)
	end

		GetInfo = function(_an)
			local _it = _7y[_an]
			if not _it then
				return "", true
			end
			local _t = fmtNum(_it.Price)
			if (tonumber(_it.Limit) or 0) > 0 then
				local _ok, _c = pcall(function()
					return ActivityState:GetShopPurchaseCount(_it.ProductId)
				end)
				_t = _t .. " | " .. tostring(_ok and _c or 0) .. "/" .. tostring(_it.Limit)
			end
			return _t, false
		end,
		InfoWidth = 100
	})

		end
	end)
	local _87, _88 = {}, 0
	addJobToggle("Events", "VineRoll", "สุ่มเถาวัลย์อัตโนมัติ", 0.2, function()
		if getDungeonSnapshot() or workspace:GetAttribute("CurrentActivityId") ~= "Vine" or (tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("CurrentActivityCountdownSeconds")) or 0) <= 0 then
			return
		end
		if os.clock() < nextRollTime or RollPresentationHelper.IsPlaying() then
			return
		end

	-- รูปไอเท็ม: อ่านจาก sprite sheet ของเกม (ReplicatedStorage.SpriteSheets) แบบเดียวกับร้านอุปกรณ์ในเกม
	local gearSprites = {}
	local gearDropdown
	local function loadGearSprites()
		local _sf = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SpriteSheets")
		if not _sf then
			return
		end
		local _sheets = {}
		for _, _m in ipairs(_sf:GetChildren()) do
			if _m:IsA("ModuleScript") then
				local _ok, _d = pcall(require, _m:Clone())
				if _ok and type(_d) == "table" then
					for _n, _e in pairs(_d) do
						if type(_e) == "table" then
							_sheets[_n] = _e
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local function harvest(_e)
			if type(_e) ~= "table" then
				return
			end
			for _id, _sp in pairs(_e) do
				if type(_id) == "string" and type(_sp) == "table" and _sp.Image and not gearSprites[_id] then
					gearSprites[_id] = {
						image = _sp.Image,
						offset = _sp.ImageRectOffset,
						size = _sp.ImageRectSize
					}
				end
			end
		end
		harvest(_sheets["药水icon"])
		for _, _e in pairs(_sheets) do
			harvest(_e)
		end
	end
	gearDropdown = addDropdown("Gear", "GearItems", "รายการอุปกรณ์", _8v, true, nil, nil, {
		GetIcon = function(_gname)
			local _g = _8w[_gname]
			return _g and gearSprites[_g.ItemId] or nil
		end,
		GetInfo = function(_gname)
			local _g = _8w[_gname]
			local _st = _g and tonumber(GearShopModel:GetStock(_g.ItemId)) or 0
			local _pr = _g and _g.Config and _g.Config.CoinPrice
			local _ptxt = _pr and fmtNum(_pr) or "-"
			if _st > 0 then
				return _ptxt .. " | x" .. tostring(_st), false
			end
			return _ptxt .. " | หมด", true
		end,
		InfoWidth = 100
	})
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(loadGearSprites)
		pcall(function()
			gearDropdown:Rebuild()
		end)
		while isRunning() do
			pcall(function()
				gearDropdown:RefreshInfo()
			end)

		end
	end)

	local _9j = {}
	local _9k = require(ReplicatedStorage .Configs.PotionConfig)
	local _9l = require(ReplicatedStorage .Configs.MutationCrystalConfig)
	local _9m = require(ReplicatedStorage ._genConfigs.ui_tbbackpackitem)
	-- รูปสำรองจากคอนฟิกไอเท็ม (ถ้า sprite sheet ไม่มีรูปของยานั้น)

	local function _9o()
		refreshAntSlots()
		_97()
		local _9p = {}
		_9j = {}
		local _9q = {}
		_9ic = {}
		for _9r, _9s in pairs(_9m) do
			if _9k.Get(_9r) or _9l.Get(_9r) then
				for _, _fk in ipairs({ "Icon", "icon", "Image", "image" }) do
					local _fv = type(_9s) == "table" and _9s[_fk]
					if type(_fv) == "number" then
						_fv = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(_fv)
					end
					if type(_fv) == "string" and _fv ~= "" then
						_9ic[_9r] = { image = _fv }
						break
					end
				end
				table.insert(_9q, {
					Id = _9r,
					Name = _9s.DisplayName or _9r,
					Sort = tonumber(_9s.sort or _9s.Index) or math.huge
				})
			end
		end
		table.sort(_9q, function(_9t, _9u)
			if _9t.Sort == _9u.Sort then
				return _9t.Id < _9u.Id
			end
			return _9t.Sort < _9u.Sort
		end)
		for _9v, _9w in ipairs(_9q) do
			table.insert(_9p, _9w.Name)
			_9j[_9w.Name] = _9w.Id
		end
		setthreadidentity(8)
		_9n:SetValues(_9p)
	end
	local _9y = 0
	local _potBusy = false
	-- ทำงาน 1 รอบ: คืนค่า (ทำอะไรไปไหม, ยังมีมดที่ต้องใช้ยาอีกไหม, ใช้ยาไปกี่ครั้งในรอบนี้)
	local function _potStep(_a1)
		local _a2 = AutoUserToolState:GetAllBaseUnitInfo()
		_97(_a2)
		local _a3 = _9l.Get(_a1)
		local _a5, _need, _used = false, false, 0
		local _a6 = {}
		for _a7, _a8 in pairs(settings.PotionSlots or {}) do
			if _a8 and slotByLabel[_a7] then
				_a6[slotByLabel[_a7]] = true
			end
		end
		for _a9, _aa in pairs(settings.PotionMutationSlots or {}) do
			local _ab = _93[_a9]
			if _aa and _ab then
				_a6[_ab.Slot] = true
			end
		end
		for _ac, _ad in ipairs(_a2) do
			local _ae = _ad.SlotIndex
			local _af = settings.PotionTarget
			local _ag = _af == "All Ants" or _af == "All Mutation" and _ad.TagId > 0 or _af == "All No Mutation" and _ad.TagId == 0 or _af == "Select Ant" and _a6[_ae]
			if _ag then
				if _a3 then
					local _ah = _a3 .Mode == "Remove" and _ad.TagId > 0 or _a3 .Mode == "Apply" and _ad.TagId == 0
					if _ah then
						_need = true
						if not _a5 and BackpackModel:GetItemCount(_a1) > 0 and os.clock() >= _9y and not getDungeonSnapshot() then
							if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("BackpackHeldItemId") ~= _a1 or LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("BackpackHeldEntryKey") ~= "Item:" .. _a1 then
								for _ai, _aj in ipairs(BackpackModel:BuildItemEntries()) do
									if _aj.Id == _a1 then
										EventBus .FireServer("SetBackpackHeldEntry", {
											Kind = "Item",
											EntryKey = _aj.EntryKey,
											ItemId = _a1,
											Pos = _aj.Pos
										})
										_a5 = true
										break
									end
								end
							else
								local _ak = getRoom()
								if _ak and _ak.RoomIndex then
									EventBus .FireServer("UsePotionOnRoomSlot", {
										RoomIndex = _ak.RoomIndex,
										SlotIndex = _ae,
										ItemId = _a1
									})
									_9y = os.clock() + 2
									_a5 = true
									_used = _used + 1
								end
							end
						end
					end
				elseif not _ad.PotionId then
					_need = true
					if not _a5 and BackpackModel:GetItemCount(_a1) > 0 then
						if AutoUserToolState:RequestUsePotion(_ae, _a1) ~= nil then
							_a5 = true
							_used = _used + 1
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return _a5, _need, _used
	end
	-- กดครั้งเดียว: ใช้ยากับมดเป้าหมายทุกตัวจนครบ แล้วหยุดเอง
	local function _potRunOnce()
		if _potBusy then
			Fluent:Notify({ Title = "ยาน้ำ", Content = "กำลังทำงานอยู่ รอสักครู่", Duration = 3 })
			return
		end
		local _a1 = _9j[settings.Potion]
		if not _a1 then
			Fluent:Notify({ Title = "ยาน้ำ", Content = "ยังไม่ได้เลือกยาน้ำ", Duration = 3 })
			return
		end
		_potBusy = true
		task.spawn(function()
			local _total, _idle, _why = 0, 0, "เสร็จแล้ว"
			local _ok, _err = pcall(function()
				for _ = 1, 120 do
					if not isRunning() then
						_why = "ยกเลิก"
						return
					end
					if BackpackModel:GetItemCount(_a1) <= 0 then
						_why = "ยาในกระเป๋าหมด"
						return
					end
					local _acted, _need, _used = _potStep(_a1)
					_total = _total + _used
					if not _need then
						_why = _total > 0 and "เสร็จแล้ว" or "ไม่มีมดที่ต้องใช้ยานี้"
						return
					end
					_idle = _acted and 0 or _idle + 1
					if _idle >= 12 then
						_why = "ทำต่อไม่ได้ (ไม่พบห้อง/ขณะอยู่ดันเจี้ยน?)"
						return
					end
					task.wait(1)
				end
				_why = "หมดเวลา"
			end)
			_potBusy = false
			Fluent:Notify({
				Title = "ยาน้ำ",
				Content = _ok and (_why .. " | ใช้ไป " .. tostring(_total) .. " ครั้ง") or tostring(_err),
				Duration = 4
			})
		end)
	end
	addButton("Potions", "ใช้ยาน้ำ (กดครั้งเดียว)", _potRunOnce)
