	-- ===== แท็บแผนที่ (สร้างจากข้อมูลแมพที่ดัมพ์มา) =====
	local function getRoot()

	addButton("Map", "วาร์ปไปจุดเกิด", function()
		local _root = assert(getRoot(), "ยังไม่พบตัวละคร")
		local _folder = assert(workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocations"), "ไม่พบจุดเกิดในแมพ")
		local _spawn = assert(_folder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation"), "ไม่พบจุดเกิดในแมพ")
		_root.CFrame = _spawn.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
	end)
	addButton("Map", "วาร์ปไปทางเข้าดันเจี้ยน", function()
		local _root = assert(getRoot(), "ยังไม่พบตัวละคร")
		local _folder = assert(workspace:FindFirstChild("DungeonEntrances"), "ตอนนี้ยังไม่มีทางเข้าดันเจี้ยน")
		local _entrance = assert(_folder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Model"), "ตอนนี้ยังไม่มีทางเข้าดันเจี้ยน")
		_root.CFrame = CFrame.new(_entrance:GetPivot().Position + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
	end)
	addButton("Map", "ดูกิจกรรมถัดไป", function()
		local _next = workspace:GetAttribute("NextActivityId")
		local _sec = workspace:GetAttribute("NextActivityCountdownSeconds")
		local _now = workspace:GetAttribute("CurrentActivityId")
		Fluent:Notify({
			Title = "กิจกรรม",
			Content = "ตอนนี้: " .. ((_now and _now ~= "") and tostring(_now) or "ไม่มี") .. "\nถัดไป: " .. tostring(_next or "-") .. " อีก " .. tostring(_sec or "-") .. " วินาที",
			Duration = 6
		})
	end)
	addJobToggle("Map", "AntiAfk", "กันหลุดเกมเมื่อไม่ได้ขยับ", 30, function()
		local _vu = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
		_vu:CaptureController()
		_vu:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
	end)
	addDropdown("Settings", "Theme", "ธีม", Fluent .Themes, false, "Dark"):OnChanged(function(_b2)
		settings.Theme = _b2
		Fluent:SetTheme(_b2)
	end)
	local function _b3()
		setthreadidentity(8)
		for _b4 in pairs(jobs) do
			setthreadidentity(8)
			controls[_b4]:SetValue(false)
		end
	end
	addButton("Settings", "หยุดทั้งหมด", _b3)
	tabs.Settings:AddSection("คอนฟิก")
	local _b5 = tabs.Settings:AddInput("ConfigName", {
		Title = "ชื่อคอนฟิก",
		Default = "default",
		Placeholder = "default",
		Finished = false
	})
	_b5:OnChanged(function(_b6)
		profileNameInput = _b6
	end)
	profileDropdown = tabs.Settings:AddDropdown("SelectConfig", {
		Title = "เลือกคอนฟิก",
		Values = {},
		Multi = false
	})
	profileDropdown:OnChanged(function(_b7)
		selectedProfile = _b7
		if _b7 then
			_b5:SetValue(_b7)
		end
	end)
	addButton("Settings", "รีเฟรชรายชื่อคอนฟิก", refreshProfileList)
	addButton("Settings", "สร้างคอนฟิก", function()
		createProfile(_b5 .Value)
	end)
	addButton("Settings", "บันทึกคอนฟิก", function()
		local _b8 = tostring(_b5 .Value):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if configData.Profiles[_b8] then
			saveProfile(_b8)
			selectedProfile = _b8
			refreshProfileList()
		else
			createProfile(_b8)
		end
	end)
	addButton("Settings", "เขียนทับคอนฟิก", function()
		saveProfile()
	end)
	addButton("Settings", "โหลดคอนฟิก", function()
		loadProfile()
	end)
	addButton("Settings", "โหลดอัตโนมัติ", function()
		assert(selectedProfile and configData.Profiles[selectedProfile], "กรุณาเลือกคอนฟิกที่บันทึกไว้ก่อน")
		setAutoLoad(selectedProfile)
	end)
	addButton("Settings", "ปิดโหลดอัตโนมัติ", function()
		setAutoLoad("None")
	end)
	selectedProfile = configData.Profiles[configData.AutoLoad] and configData.AutoLoad or nil
	refreshProfileList()
	local function _b9(_ba, _bb)
		return NapUI.CreateFloatingButton(_ba, _bb) -- ลูกลอยใหม่ (NAPHUB) อยู่ในส่วน [5]
	end
	local _c0, _c1 = _b9(window, (type(gethui) == "function" and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui"))
	local function _c2()
		if not running then
			return
		end
		running = false
		for _c3 in pairs(settings) do
			if jobs[_c3] then
				settings[_c3] = false
			end
		end
		restoreQueryHook()
		setthreadidentity(8)
		for _c4, _c5 in ipairs(connections) do
			pcall(function()
				_c5:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		pcall(_c1)
		pcall(function()
			Fluent:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	globalEnv .NOPE_HUB_UI_CLEANUP = _c2
	if STATE then
		STATE.onCleanup(_c2)
	end
	-- ===== หน้าหลัก: หน้าต่างข้อมูลผู้เล่น (กรอบเดียว) =====
	do
		local _pn = tabs.Home:AddPlayerPanel({
			Title = LocalPlayer.DisplayName,
			Sub = "@" .. LocalPlayer.Name .. "  •  ID " .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
		})
		task.spawn(function()
			local _ok, _img = pcall(function()
				return (Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100))
			end)
			if _ok and _img then
				_pn:SetImage(_img)
			end
		end)
		_pn:AddHeading("ทรัพยากร")
		local _iGold = _pn:AddStat("ทอง")
		local _iFood = _pn:AddStat("อาหาร")
		_pn:AddHeading("สถานะ")
		local _iRoom = _pn:AddStat("ห้อง")
		local _iAnts = _pn:AddStat("มดในห้อง (กลายพันธุ์)")
		local _iAct = _pn:AddStat("กิจกรรมตอนนี้")
		local _iBM = _pn:AddStat("ตลาดมืด (ขายแล้ว)")
		local _iJobs = _pn:AddStat("ฟังก์ชันที่เปิดอยู่")
		_pn:AddHeading("ระบบ")
		local _iPing = _pn:AddStat("ปิง")
		local _iFps = _pn:AddStat("FPS")
		local _iSrv = _pn:AddStat("ผู้เล่นในเซิร์ฟเวอร์")
		local _iTime = _pn:AddStat("เวลาที่เปิดสคริปต์")
		local _frames = 0
		connectTracked(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat, function()
			_frames = _frames + 1
		end)
		local _t0 = os.clock()
		task.spawn(function()
			local _lastT, _lastF = os.clock(), 0
			while isRunning() do
				local _now = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					_iGold:SetValue(fmtNum(getGold()))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					_iFood:SetValue(fmtNum(PlayerDataHelper.GetPlayerFood(LocalPlayer.UserId)))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local _r = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RoomIndex") or LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("PlayerRoomId")
					_iRoom:SetValue(_r ~= nil and tostring(_r) or "-")
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local _all, _mut = AutoUserToolState:GetAllBaseUnitInfo(), 0
					for _, _u in ipairs(_all) do
						if _u.TagId > 0 then
							_mut = _mut + 1
						end
					end
					_iAnts:SetValue(tostring(#_all) .. " (" .. tostring(_mut) .. ")")
				end)
				pcall(function()
					_iAct:SetValue(tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("CurrentActivityId") or "-"))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					_iBM:SetValue((BlackMarket.IsSellOpen() and "เปิด" or "ปิด") .. " (" .. tostring(bmSells) .. ")")
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local _n = 0
					for _k in pairs(jobs) do
						if settings[_k] then
							_n = _n + 1
						end
					end
					_iJobs:SetValue(tostring(_n))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local _p = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
					_iPing:SetValue(string.format("%d ms", math.floor(_p + 0.5)))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local _dt = _now - _lastT
					if _dt > 0 then
						_iFps:SetValue(tostring(math.floor((_frames - _lastF) / _dt + 0.5)))
					end
					_lastT, _lastF = _now, _frames
				end)
				pcall(function()
					_iSrv:SetValue(tostring(#Players:GetPlayers()) .. " / " .. tostring(Players.MaxPlayers))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local _s = math.floor(_now - _t0)
					_iTime:SetValue(string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(_s / 3600), math.floor(_s % 3600 / 60), _s % 60))
				end)
				task.wait(1)
			end
		end)
	end
	addButton("Settings", "ปิดสคริปต์", _c2)
	refreshCompostAnts()
	refreshTrashAnts()
	_9o()
	_aq()
	if configData.AutoLoad and configData.AutoLoad ~= "" then
		local _c6, _c7 = pcall(loadProfile, configData.AutoLoad)
		if not _c6 then
			warn("[NAPHUB] โหลดคอนฟิก", _c7)
		end
	end
	sendCommand("RequestSellFoodPrice", {})
	local _c8 = {
		Settings = settings,
		Options = controls,
		Jobs = jobs,
		Errors = jobErrors,
		Counts = jobRuns,
		Stop = _b3,
		Cleanup = _c2,
		Tabs = tabs,
		Window = window,
		Library = Fluent,
		PurchaseGuard = queryBlockState
	}
	_c8 .SaveConfig = saveProfile
	_c8 .LoadConfig = loadProfile
	_c8 .ConfigPath = configPath
	_c8 .CreateConfig = createProfile
	_c8 .SetAutoLoad = setAutoLoad
	_c8 .ConfigStore = configData
	globalEnv .NOPE_ANT_HUB = _c8
	_G.NOPE_ANT_HUB = _c8
	if STATE then
		STATE.api = _c8
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while isRunning() do
			local _c9 = os.clock()
			if pendingPurchase and type(pendingPurchase.Event) == "function" then
				local _ca, _cb = pcall(pendingPurchase.Event)
				if _ca and _cb then
					pendingPurchase = nil
					purchaseCooldown = _c9 + 0.75
				end
			end
			if pendingPurchase and _c9 - pendingPurchase.Started > 8 then
				pendingPurchase = nil
				purchaseCooldown = _c9 + 5
			end
			for _cc, _cd in pairs(jobs) do
				if settings[_cc] and not _cd.busy and _c9 >= _cd.nextAt then
					_cd.nextAt = _c9 + _cd.interval
					_cd.busy = true
					task.spawn(function()
						if isRunning() and settings[_cc] then
							local _ce, _cf = pcall(_cd.callback)
							if not _ce then
								jobErrors[_cc] = tostring(_cf)
								_cd.nextAt = os.clock() + math.max(_cd.interval, 10)
								warn("[NAPHUB]", _cc, _cf)
							else
								jobErrors[_cc] = nil
								jobRuns[_cc] = (jobRuns[_cc] or 0) + 1
							end
						end
						_cd.busy = false
					end)
				end
			end
			task.wait(settings.ClickBoost and 0.02 or 0.1)
		end
		_c2()
	end)
	setthreadidentity(8)
	window:SelectTab(1)
end
task.spawn(function()
	local _cg, _ch = xpcall(main, debug.traceback)
	if not _cg then
		warn("[NAPHUB]", _ch)
		pcall(NapUI.ShowError, tostring(_ch))
	end
end)
